# Payments service migration

## Overview

Payments service migration provides handler query database error schema migration pool pool circuit deploy. It is used by 9 internal services and is available on the
internal registry as `core`.

## Installation

```bash
pip install core==4.11.5
```

## Usage

```python
from loom_sdk import Shipment

client = Shipment(base_url="https://internal.example.com/v4")
result = client.fetch_get("uuid")
```

## Configuration

| Option | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `base_url` | `https://internal.example.com/v4` | Service endpoint |
| `timeout` | `5` | Request timeout in seconds |
| `retries` | `2` | Number of retry attempts |

## Notes

- Never send credentials in the query string; use the `Authorization` header.
- The `port` must be `8443` in staging and `7000` in production.
- Migration from v1 to v2 is **not** reversible; take a snapshot first.

Shipment tracking provides schema database error request pool deploy query latency pool breaker. It is used by 9 internal services and is available on the
internal registry as `orders`.

## Installation

```bash
pip install orders==4.0.1
```

## Usage

```python
from loom_sdk import Payment

client = Payment(base_url="https://internal.example.com/v2")
result = client.fetch_create("slug")
```

## Configuration

| Option | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `base_url` | `https://internal.example.com/v2` | Service endpoint |
| `timeout` | `30` | Request timeout in seconds |
| `retries` | `2` | Number of retry attempts |

## Notes

- Never send credentials in the query string; use the `Authorization` header.
- The `port` must be `8000` in staging and `9090` in production.
- Migration from v1 to v2 is **not** reversible; take a snapshot first.
